aruṇakirinātar aruḷiya tiruppukaḻ pāṭalkaḷ ( 1 - 330 )

pāṭal 1 -- vināyakar tuti

rākam - nāṭṭi; tāḷam - āti
tattaṉa taṉataṉa tattaṉa taṉataṉa
tattaṉa taṉataṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
kittala niṟikaṉi appamo ṭavalpori
kappiya karimukaṉ ...... aṭipēṇik
kaṟṟiṭum aṭiyavar puttiyil uṟipava
kaṟpakam eṉaviṉi ...... kaṭitēkum
mattamu matiyamum vittiṭum araṉmakaṉ
maṟporu tiraḷpuya ...... matayāṉi
mattaḷa vayiṟaṉi uttami putalvaṉi
maṭṭaviḻ malarkoṭu ...... paṇivēṉē
muttamiḻ aṭiviṉi muṟpaṭu kiritaṉil
muṟpaṭa eḻutiya ...... mutalvōṉē
muppuram ericeyta accivaṉ uṟiratam
accatu poṭiceyta ...... atitīrā
attuya ratukoṭu cuppira maṇipaṭum
appuṉa mataṉiṭi ...... ipamāki
akkuṟa makaḷuṭaṉ acciṟu murukaṉi
akkaṇa maṇamaruḷ ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 2 vināyakar tuti

rākam - nāṭṭi / mōkaṉam; tāḷam - āti
tattataṉa tattataṉa tattataṉa tattataṉa
tattataṉa tattataṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
pakkarivi citramaṇi poṟkalaṇi yiṭṭanaṭi
paṭciyeṉu mukratura ...... kamunīpap
pakkuvama larttoṭiyum akkuvaṭu paṭṭoḻiya
paṭṭuruva viṭṭaruḷki ...... vaṭivēlum
tikkatuma tikkavaru kukkuṭamum raṭcitaru
ciṟṟaṭiyu muṟṟiyapa ...... ṉirutōḷum
ceyppatiyum vittuyarti ruppukaḻvi ruppamoṭu
ceppeṉe ṉakkaruḷki ...... maṟavēṉē
ikkavari naṟkaṉikaḷ carkkaripa ruppuṭaṉey
eṭporiya vaṟṟuvari ...... iḷanīrvaṇ
ṭeccilpaya ṟappavaki paccarici piṭṭuveḷa
rippaḻami ṭippalvaki ...... taṉa஧mulam
mikkaaṭi ciṟkaṭali paṭcaṇame ṉakkoḷoru
vikkinaca marttaṉeṉum ...... aruḷāḻi
veṟpakuṭi laccaṭila viṟparama rappararuḷ
vittakama ruppuṭiya ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 3 vināyakar

rākam - hamsatvaṉi / āṉantapiravi; tāḷam - aṅkatāḷam (8)
takatakiṭa-2 1/2, takatakiṭa-2 1/2, takatimitaka-3
tantataṉat tāṉataṉat ...... taṉatāṉa
umpartarut tēnumaṇik ...... kacivāki
oṇkaṭaliṟ ṟēṉamutat ...... tuṇarvūṟi
iṉparacat tēparukip ...... palakālum
eṉṟaṉuyirk kātaravuṟ ...... ṟaruḷvāyē
tampitaṉak kākavaṉat ...... taṇivōṉē
tantivalat tālaruḷkik ...... kaṉiyōṉē
aṉpartamak kāṉanilip ...... poruḷōṉē
aintukarat tāṉimukap ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 4 vināyakar

rākam - hamsatvaṉi; tāḷam - aṅkatāḷam (7 1/2)
takiṭa-1 1/2, takatimi-2, takatimi-2, takajaṉu-2
taṉaṉa taṉataṉa tattaṉa tattaṉa
taṉaṉa taṉataṉa ttataṉa tattaṉa
taṉaṉa taṉataṉa ttataṉa tattaṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
niṉatu tiruvaṭi cattima yiṟkoṭi
niṉivu karutiṭu puttiko ṭuttiṭa
niṟiya amutucey muppaḻa mappamu ...... nikaḻpāltēṉ
neṭiya vaḷimuṟi ikkoṭu laṭṭukam
niṟavil aricipa ruppaval eṭpori
nikaril iṉikata likkaṉi varkkamum ...... iḷanīrum
maṉatu makiḻvoṭu toṭṭaka rattoru
makara calaniti vittatu tikkara
vaḷaru karimuka oṟṟima ruppaṉi ...... valamāka
maruvu malarpuṉi tottira coṟkoṭu
vaḷarki kuḻipiṭi toppaṇa kuṭṭoṭu
vaṉaca paripura poṟpata arccaṉi ...... maṟavēṉē
teṉaṉa teṉateṉa tetteṉa ṉappala
ciṟiya aṟupata moyttuti rappuṉal
tiraḷum uṟucati pittani ṇakkuṭal ...... ceṟa஧muḷi
ceruma utarani rappuce rukkuṭal
niriya aravani ṟittaka ḷattiṭi
timita timitimi mattaḷi ṭakkikaḷ ...... cekacēcē
eṉave tukutuku tuttateṉa ottukaḷ
tuṭikaḷ iṭimika ottumu ḻakkiṭa
ṭimuṭa ṭimuṭimu ṭiṭṭime ṉattavil ...... eḻumōci
ikali alakikaḷ kippaṟi koṭṭiṭa
iraṇa payiravi cuṟṟuna ṭittiṭa
etiru nicicara rippeli yiṭṭaruḷ ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 5 vināyakar

rākam - keḷaḷi; tāḷam - tisratrupuṭi (7) / misracāpu (3 1/2)
taṉataṉaṉa tāṉa taṉataṉaṉa tāṉa
taṉataṉaṉa tāṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
viṭamaṭicu vēli amararpaṭi cūlam
viciyaṉviṭu pāṇa ...... meṉavētāṉ
viḻiyumati pāra vitamumuṭi mātar
viṉiyiṉ viḷi vētum ...... aṟiyātē
kaṭiyulavu pāyal pakalirave ṉātu
kalavitaṉil mūḻki ...... vaṟitāya
kayavaṉaṟi vīṉaṉ ivaṉumuyar nīṭu
kaḻaliṇikaḷ cēra ...... aruḷvāyē
iṭiyarciṟu pāli tiruṭikoṭu pōka
iṟivaṉmakaḷ vāymi ...... aṟiyātē
itayamika vāṭi yuṭiyapiḷi nāta
kaṇapatiye ṉāma ...... muṟikūṟa
aṭiyalavar āvi veruvaaṭi kūra
acalumaṟi yāmal ...... avarōṭa
akalvateṉa ṭācol eṉavumuṭi cāṭa
aṟivaruḷum āṉi ...... mukavōṉē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 6 (nūl)

rākam - keḷaḷi; tāḷam - tisratrupuṭi (7) / misracāpu (3 1/2)
tattattaṉa tattat taṉataṉa
tattattaṉa tattat taṉataṉa
tattattaṉa tattat taṉataṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
muttittaru pattit tirunaki
attikkiṟi cattic caravaṇa
muttikkoru vittuk kurupara ...... eṉavōtum
mukkaṭpara maṟkuc curutiyiṉ
muṟpaṭṭatu kaṟpit tiruvarum
muppattumu varkkat tamararum ...... aṭipēṇap
pattuttali tattak kaṇitoṭu
oṟṟikkiri mattip porutoru
paṭṭappakal vaṭṭat tikiriyil ...... iravākap
pattaṟkira tattik kaṭaviya
paccippuyal meccat takuporuḷ
paṭcattoṭu raṭcit taruḷvatum ...... orunāḷē
tittitteya ottap paripura
nirttappatam vittup payiravi
tikkoṭkana ṭikkak kaḻukoṭu ...... kaḻutāṭat
tikkuppari aṭṭap payiravar
tokkuttoku tokkut tokutoku
citrappavu rikkut trikaṭaka ...... eṉavōtak
kottuppaṟi koṭṭak kaḷamici
kukkukkuku kukkuk kukukuku
kuttipputi pukkup piṭiyeṉa ...... mutukūki
kōṭpuṟṟeḻa naṭpaṟ ṟavuṇari
veṭṭippali yiṭṭuk kulakiri
kuttuppaṭa ottup poravala ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 7 (tirupparaṅkuṉṟam)

rākam - .....; tāḷam - .........
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
arukku maṅkiyar malaraṭi varuṭiye
karutta ṟintupiṉ aritaṉil uṭitaṉi
aviḻttum aṅkuḷa aracili taṭaviyum ...... irutōḷuṟ
ṟaṇittum aṅkiyiṉ aṭitoṟum nakameḻa
utaṭṭi meṉṟupal iṭukuṟi kaḷumiṭa
aṭikka ḷantaṉil mayilkuyil puṟaveṉa ...... mikavāyviṭ
ṭurukkum aṅkiyiṉ meḻukeṉa urukiya
ciratti miñciṭum anupavam uṟupalam
uṟakki yiṉkaṉi nikareṉa ilakiya ...... mulimēlvīḻn
turukka laṅkimey urukiṭa amutuku
perutta untiyiṉ muḻukime yuṇarvaṟa
uḻitti ṭuṅkaṉa kalaviyi makiḻvatu ...... tavirvēṉō
irukku mantiram eḻuvaki munipeṟa
uritta camprama caravaṇa pavakuka
itatta iṅkitam ilakiya aṟumuka ...... eḻilvēḷeṉ
ṟilakka ṇaṅkaḷum iyalici kaḷumika
virikkum ampala maturita kavitaṉi
iyaṟṟu centamiḻ vitamoṭu puyamici ...... puṉivōṉē
cerukkum ampala micitaṉil acivuṟa
naṭitta caṅkarar vaḻivaḻi aṭiyavar
tirukku runtaṭi aruḷpeṟa aruḷiya ...... kurunātar
tirukku ḻantiyu meṉaavar vaḻipaṭu
kurukka ḷiṉtiṟa meṉavaru periyava
tiruppa raṅkiri taṉiluṟi caravaṇa ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 8 (tirupparaṅkuṉṟam)

rākam - sāvēri; tāḷam - āti
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
uṉitti ṉantoḻu tilaṉuṉa tiyalpiṉi
uritti laṉpala malarkoṭuṉ aṭiyiṇi
uṟippa ṇintilaṉ orutava milaṉuṉa ...... taruḷmāṟā
uḷattu ḷaṉpiṉar uṟiviṭam aṟikilaṉ
viruppo ṭuṉcika ramumvalam varukilaṉ
uvappo ṭuṉpukaḻ tuticeya viḻikilaṉ ...... malipōlē
kaṉitte ḻumpaka ṭatupiṭar micivaru
kaṟutta veñciṉa maṟalitaṉ uḻiyiṉar
katitta ṭarnteṟi kayiṟaṭu katikoṭu ...... porupōtē
kalakku ṟuñceyal oḻivaṟa aḻivuṟu
karuttu nintala muṟupoḻu taḷavikoḷ
kaṇattil eṉpaya maṟamayil mutukiṉil ...... varuvāyē
viṉitta lantaṉil alakikaḷ kutikoḷa
viḻukku ṭintumey ukutaci kaḻukuṇa
viritta kuñciyar eṉumavu ṇariamar ...... purivēlā
mikutta paṇpayil kuyilmoḻi aḻakiya
koṭicci kuṅkuma mulimuka ṭuḻunaṟi
viritta cantaṉa mrukamata puyavari ...... uṭiyōṉē
tiṉatti ṉañcatur maṟimuni muṟikoṭu
puṉaṟco rintalar potiyavi ṇavaroṭu
ciṉatti nintaṉi ceyumuni varartoḻa ...... makiḻvōṉē
teṉatte ṉantaṉa eṉavari yaḷinaṟi
teviṭṭa aṉpoṭu parukuyar poḻiltikaḻ
tirup paraṅkiri taṉiluṟi caravaṇa ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 9 (tirupparaṅkuṉṟam)

rākam - hintōḷam / varāḷi; tāḷam - aṅkatāḷam (7) (tisratrupuṭi)
takiṭa-1 1/2, takiṭa-1 1/2, takatakiṭa-2 1/2, takiṭa-1 1/2
taṉaṉatanta tattatta tanta
taṉaṉatanta tattatta tanta
taṉaṉatanta tattatta tanta ...... taṉatāṉa
karuvaṭintu pattuṟṟa tiṅkaḷ
vayiṟiruntu muṟṟippa yiṉṟu
kaṭiyilvantu tittukku ḻanti ...... vaṭivākik
kaḻuviyaṅke ṭuttuccu ranta
muliyaruntu vikkakki ṭantu
karaṟiyaṅki koṭṭitta vaḻntu ...... naṭamāṭi
arivaṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭicca taṅki
iṭukutampi poṟcuṭṭi taṇṭi
aviyaṇintu muṟṟikki ḷarntu ...... vayatēṟi
ariyapeṇkaḷ naṭpippu ṇarntu
piṇiyuḻaṉṟu cuṟṟitti rinta
tamiyumuṉkru piccittam eṉṟu ...... peṟuvēṉō
iraviintraṉ veṟṟikku raṅki
ṉaracareṉṟum oppaṟṟa unti
yiṟivaṉeṇki ṉakkartta ṉeṉṟum ...... neṭunīlaṉ
eriyateṉṟum rutraṟci ṟanta
anumaṉeṉṟum oppaṟṟa aṇṭar
evaruminta varkkattil vantu ...... puṉamēva
ariyataṉpa ṭikkartta reṉṟu
acurartaṅki ḷikkaṭṭi veṉṟa
arimukuntaṉ meccuṟṟa paṇpiṉ ...... marukōṉē
ayaṉiyumpu ṭittucci ṉantu
ulakamumpa ṭittuppa rintu
aruḷparaṅki rikkuṭci ṟanta ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 10 (tirupparaṅkuṉṟam)

rākam - ....; tāḷam - .......
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa
taṉatta tantaṉa taṉataṉa taṉataṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
kaṟukkum añcaṉa viḻiyiṇi ayilkoṭu
nerukki neñcaṟa eṟitaru poḻutoru
kaṉikkuḷ iṉcuvi aḻutukum oruciṟu ...... nakiyālē
kaḷakko ḻuṅkali valikoṭu viciṟiye
maṉikke ḻuntirum eṉamaṉam urukor
kavaṟci koṇṭiṭa maṉitaṉil aḻakoṭu ...... koṭupōki
naṟitta pañcaṇi miciyiṉil maṉamuṟa
aṇitta kantaṉil iṇimuli etirpora
nakatta ḻuntiṭa amutitaḻ parukiya ...... miṭaṟu஡ṭē
naṭitte ḻuṅkural kumukumu kumuveṉa
icittu naṉkoṭu maṉamatu maṟukiṭa
naḻuppu nañcaṉa ciṟumikaḷ tuyaraṟa ...... aruḷvāyē
niṟitta teṇṭiri mokumoku mokuveṉa
uratta kañcuki muṭineṟu neṟuveṉa
niṟitta aṇṭamu kaṭukiṭu kiṭuveṉa ...... varipōlum
nivatta tiṇtikaḻ nicicara ruramoṭu
cirakko ṭuṅkuvi malipuri tariru
niṇakku ḻampoṭu kurutikaḷ coritara ...... aṭutīrā
tiṟaṟka ruṅkuḻal umiyavaḷ aruḷuṟu
puḻikki taṇkaṭa kayamuka mikavuḷa
civakko ḻuntaṉa kaṇapati yuṭaṉvarum ...... iḷiyōṉē
ciṉatto ṭuñcamaṉ utipaṭa niṟuviya
paraṟku ḷaṉpuṟu putalvanaṉ maṇiyuku
tiruppa raṅkiri taṉiluṟi caravaṇa ...... perumāḷē.

pāṭal 11 (tirupparaṅkuṉṟam)

rākam - caṅkarāparaṇam / nīlāmpari; tāḷam - tisratrupuṭi (7)
taṉatantaṉa tantaṉa tantaṉa
taṉatantaṉa tantaṉa tantaṉa
taṉatantaṉa tantaṉa tantaṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
kaṉakantiraḷ kiṉṟape ruṅkiri
taṉilvantuta kaṉtakaṉ eṉṟiṭu
katirmiñciya ceṇṭie ṟintiṭu ...... katiyōṉē
kaṭamiñci anantavi tampuṇar
kavaḷantaṉi uṇṭuva ḷarntiṭu
kariyiṉṟuṇi eṉṟupi ṟantiṭu ...... murukōṉē
paṉakantuyil kiṉṟati ṟampuṉi
kaṭalmuṉpuka ṭintapa ramparar
paṭarumpuyal eṉṟavar aṉpukoḷ ...... marukōṉē
palatuṉpamuḻaṉṟuka laṅkiya
ciṟiyaṉpuli yaṉkoli yaṉpuri
pavamiṉṟuka ḻintiṭa vantaruḷ ...... purivāyē
aṉakaṉpeyar niṉṟuru ḷuntiri
puramuntiri veṉṟiṭa iṉpuṭaṉ
aḻaluntana kuntiṟal koṇṭavar ...... putalvōṉē
aṭalvantumu ḻaṅkiyi ṭumpaṟi
ṭuṭuṭuṇṭuṭu ṭuṇṭuṭu ṭuṇṭeṉa
atirkiṉṟiṭa aṇṭane rintiṭa ...... varucūrar
maṉamuntaḻal ceṉṟiṭa aṉṟavar
uṭaluṅkuṭa luṅkiḻi koṇṭiṭa
mayilveṉṟaṉil vantaru ḷuṅkaṉa ...... periyōṉē
matiyuṅkati runtaṭa vumpaṭi
uyarkiṉṟava ṉaṅkaḷpo runtiya
vaḷamoṉṟupa raṅkiri vantaruḷ ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 12 (tirupparaṅkuṉṟam)

rākam - ....; tāḷam - .......
tāṉaṉa tantaṉa tantaṉan tantaṉa
tāṉaṉa tantaṉa tantaṉan tantaṉa
tāṉaṉa tantaṉa tantaṉan tantaṉa ...... taṉatāṉa
kātaṭa ruṅkayal koṇṭicin timpoṟi
vāḷima yaṅkama ṉampayan tantiruḷ
kāltara vintuvi cumpilaṅ kumpoḻu ...... torukōṭi
kāykati reṉṟoḷir ceñcilam puṅkaṇi
yāḻiyu ṭaṉkaṭa kantulaṅ kumpaṭi
kāmaṉe ṭuñcili koṇṭaṭarn tumporu ...... mayalālē
vātupu rintavar ceṅkitan tiṅkita
mākana ṭantavar piṉtirin tuntaṉa
mārpila ḻuntaa ṇintiṭun tuṉpama ...... tuḻalātē
vācami kuntaka ṭampameṉ kiṇkiṇi
mālika raṅkoḷum aṉparvan taṉpoṭu
vāḻani tampuṉi yumpatan tantuṉa ...... taruḷtārāy
pōtilu ṟintaruḷ kiṉṟavaṉ ceñcira
mītuta ṭintuvi laṅkiṭum puṅkava
pōtava ḷañciva caṅkaraṉ koṇṭiṭa ...... moḻivōṉē
pūkamu ṭaṉtikaḻ caṅkiṉaṅ koṇṭaki
஡ṇavama ṭantipu rantaraṉ tantaruḷ
pūvika ruṅkuṟa miṉkalan taṅkupa ...... ṉirutōḷā
tītaka moṉṟiṉar vañcakan tuñciyi
ṭātavar caṅkarar tantateṉ pumpala

tutaṟumoru kalapimici vanteḻun taṇṭartam
pakiyacura raṉivaruṭal cantucan tuṅkatañ ...... cintumvēlā
paṭiyavarum imiyavarum niṉṟiṟiñ ceṇkuṇaṉ
paḻiyiṟi yuruvamili aṉparpaṅ kaṉperum
paruvaralcey purameriya viṇṭiṭuñ ceṅkaṇaṇ ...... kaṅkimāṉvāḻ
caṭilamici aḻakupuṉi koṉṟiyum paṇpuṟun
taruṇamati yiṉakuṟicey tuṇṭamuñ ceṅkiyoṇ
cakalapuva ṉamumoḻika taṅkuṟaṅ kaṅkiyum ...... poṅkinīṭum
caṭamaruvu viṭiyaravar tuṅkaam paṅkiṉiṉ
ṟulakutaru kavuriyumi koṅkitan taṉpuṟun
tamiḻviraka uyarparama caṅkaraṉ kumpiṭun ...... tampirāṉē.

pāṭal 53 (tiruccentūr)

vārum vīṭeyeṉ ṟōtitam pāyalmici ...... yaṉpuḷārpōl
vāca pācakañ cūtupan tāṭiḻi
vērvi pāyacin tākukoñ cāraviḻi
vāku tōḷkarañ cērvitan tāṭumavar ...... cantamāmō
tīta tōtakan tītatin tōtitimi
ṭūṭu ṭūṭuṭuṇ ṭūṭuṭuṇ ṭūṭuṭuṭu
ṭīku ṭīkukam pōlavoṇ pērimura ...... caṅkaḷvīṟac
cēṭaṉ mēruvuñ cūraṉun tārukaṉum
vīḻa ēḻtaṭan tūḷikoṇ ṭāṭamarar
cēce cēceyeṉ ṟāṭaniṉ ṟāṭiviṭu ...... maṅkivēlā
tāti kātilaṅ kōtuciṅ kāramuka
māṟum vākuvuṅ kūracan tāṉacuka
tāri mārpalaṅ kāriyeṉ pāvivaḷi ...... yeṅkaḷmātit
tāru pāḷitañ cōracin tāmaṇika
ḷāṭa vēpuṇarn tāṭivaṅ kāramoṭu
tāḻi vāṉuyarn tāṭucen tūriluṟi ...... tampirāṉē.

pāṭal 81 (tiruccentūr)

pāṭal 113 ( paḻani )

pāṭal 115 ( paḻani )

rākam - ....; tāḷam -
tattā taṉattataṉa tattā taṉattataṉa
tāṉat taṉantataṉa tāṉat taṉantataṉa ...... taṉataṉatāṉa
ittā raṇikkuḷmanu vittāy muḷittaḻuta
kēvik kiṭantumaṭi mītiṟ ṟavaḻntaṭikaḷ
tattā taṉattataṉa iṭṭē teruttaliyil
ōṭit tirintunava kōṭip prapantakali
yiccīr payiṟṟavaya teṭṭoṭu meṭṭuvara
vālak kuṇaṅkaḷpayil kōlap petumpiyarka ...... ḷuṭaṉuṟavāki
ikkār carattumata ṉukkē iḷittuveku
vākak kalampavaki pāṭip pukaḻntupala

tēṭic cukantaaṇi mītiṟ ṟuyiṉṟucuka
miṭṭā taratturuki vaṭṭār mulikkuḷiṭi
muḻkik kiṭantumaya lākit tuḷintucila ...... piṇiyamuṭic
cattāṉa puttiyatu keṭṭē kiṭakkanama
ṉōṭit toṭarntukayi ṟāṭik koḷumpoḻutu
peṟṟōrkaḷ cuṟṟiyaḻa vuṟṟārkaḷ mettaaḻa
ūruk kaṭaṅkalilar kālaṟ kaṭaṅkavuyir
takkā tivarkkumaya ṉiṭṭāṉ vitippaṭiyi
ṉōlip paḻampaṭiyi ṉāliṟ ṟiṟantateṉa ...... eṭumeṉavōṭic
caṭṭā navappaṟikaḷ koṭṭā variccuṭali
yēkic caṭamperitu vēkap puṭañcamiya
iṭṭē yaṉaṟkuḷeri paṭṭā reṉattaḻuvi
nīriṟ paṭintuviṭu pācat takaṉṟuṉatu
caṟpō takappatuma muṟṟē tamiḻkkaviti
pēcip paṇinturuku nēcatti yiṉṟutara ...... iṉivaravēṇum
tittā tirittikuṭa tattā taṉattakuta
tātat taṉantataṉa tāṉat taṉantataṉa
ceccē cekuccekuku tittā timittatiku
tātatta centikuta tītatta centarika
tittā kiṭakkaṇaka ṭakkā kukukkukuku
tōtak kaṇaṅkaṇaka kūkuk kiṇaṅkiṇeṉa ...... orumayilēṟit
tiṭṭē ratattacurar paṭṭē viḻapporutu
vēlit toḷintuvari yēḻip piḷantuvaru
cittā parattamarar kattā kuṟattimuli
mītiṟ puṇarntucuka līlik katampamaṇi
cuttā vumikkumoru muttāy muḷittakuru
nātak kuḻantiyeṉa vōṭik kaṭampamalar ...... aṇitirumārpā
mattā matakkaḷiṟu piṟṟā ṉutittakuka
ṉētat tilaṅkiyiṉi lātikka muṇṭatoru
muṭṭā ḷarakkartali yiṟṟē viḻakkaṇika
ḷētoṭṭa koṇṭaluru vākic cumantatika
maṭṭār malarkkamala muṟṟā caṉattiruvi
mārpiṟ puṇarntaraku rāmaṟku maṉpuṭiya ...... marumakaṉāki
vaṟṟā matukkaruṇi yuṟṟē maṟikkalika
ḷōtit terintutamiḻ cōtit talaṅkalaṇi
yattā parattiyaṟi vittāvi cuṟṟumoḷi
yākip prapantamaṇi vēltoṭṭa mintapuya
varkkā maruppuḻuku muṭṭā tiruppaḻani
vāḻavuk kukantaṭiya rāvikkuḷ niṉṟulavi ...... varuperumāḷē.

pāṭal 121 ( paḻani )

rākam - ....; tāḷam -

pāṭal 150 ( paḻani )

rākam - ....; tāḷam -
tantam tantam tantam tantam

kuṭiyeṉa vaḷartaru koṭiyavar kaṭiyavar

eṉṟeṉ ṟuṅkaṉ ṟuntuṉ puṅkoṇ

toḻumaki miyiṉili yuṇarviṉi ṉaruḷpeṟa
iṉpum paṇpun tempuñ campan
tamumika varuḷpeṟa viṭitaru vitamuṉa ...... maruḷvāyē
eṅkuṅ kañcaṉ vañcaṉ koñcaṉ
avaṉviṭu maticaya viṉiyuṟu malakiyi
veṉṟuṅ koṉṟun tuṇṭan tuṇṭañ

tuyilpava ṉekiṉaṉi yutaviya karumukil ...... marukōṉē
oṉṟeṉ ṟeṉṟun tuṉṟuṅ kuṉṟun
toḷipaṭa matakari mukaṉuṭal neripaṭa
ṭuṇṭuṇ ṭuṇṭuṇ ṭiṇṭiṇ ṭiṇṭiṇ
ṭiṭiyeṉa viḻumeḻu paṭikaḷu matirpaṭa
oṇcaṅ kañcañ cañcañ cañceṉ
ṟoliceya makapati tuticeya acurari ...... yaṭuvōṉē
untaṉ tañcan tañcan tañcañ
civaṉaruḷ kurupara veṉamuni vararpaṇi
yunton tanton tanton tanteṉ
ṟolipaṭa naṭamiṭu paraṉaru ḷaṟumuka
uṇkaṇ vaṇṭuṅ koṇṭun taṅkum
viripaṭu kuravala ralartaru meḻilpuṉi ...... puyavīrā
aṉṟeṉ ṟoṉṟuṅ koṇṭaṉ piṉṟaṅ
kaṭiyavar tamiyikaḻ camaṇarkaḷ kaḻuviṉil
aṅkañ cintum paṅkan tuñcum
paṭiyoru tokutiyi ṉurinati yetirpaṭa
aṉpiṉ paṇpeṅ kuṅkaṇ ṭeṉpiṉ
ariviyi yetirvara viṭukavi pukaltaru ...... tiṟalōṉē
aṇṭaṅ kaṇṭum paṇṭuṇ ṭumpoṅ
kamartaṉil vijayava ṉiratami naṭaviya
tuṅkaṉ vañcaṉ caṅkaṉ mintaṉ
tarumakaṉ munitaḻal varutaka rivarvala
aṅkaṅ kañcañ caṅkam poṅkuṅ
kayaniṟi vaḷamuṟu civakiri maruviya ...... perumāḷē.

patami cinteḻu lōkamu mēvalam
noṭiyil vantiṭu māmayil mītoru

nilaviyal cērmuka matiluyar māmayal

araṉiṭa pākama ...... tuṟicōti
amiyumi ṭākiṉi tiripuri nāraṇi
aḻakiya mātaruḷ ...... putalvōṉē
kuravaṇi pūṣaṇa caravaṇa tēcika
kukakaru ṇāniti ...... amarēcā
kuṟamaka ḷāṉimiṉ maruviya pūraṇa
kurukiri mēviya ...... perumāḷē.

taritta rittari taririri riririri

teṟitti ṭakkaḻu naritiṉa niṇamici ...... porumvēlā

ticitoṟuṅ kaṟpik kikkiṉi yaṟpan

karimukaṉ citrap poṟpukar veṟpaṉ ...... ṟaṉiyīṉum

paḻayaaṇ ṭattip peṟṟama ṭappeṇ ...... paṇivārip

pavatikam parkkup pukkavaḷ pakkam
payilvaram peṟṟuk kacciyil niṟkum ...... perumāḷē.

pāṭal 325 ( kāñcīpuram )

rākam - ......; tāḷam - .....

perukkapput taṭakkikkaṟ pakattoppik kaṇattukkup
piracittak koṭikkukṭak ...... koṭiyōṉē
paṟikkoṭṭik kaḷiccuṟṟak kuṟaṭcekkaṭ kaṇattiṟkup
palikkuppac cuṭaṟkuttip ...... pakirvēlā
paṇappattik kaṇattuttip paṭukkikkac capatticcip
paṭukkaccip paticcokkap ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 326 ( kāñcīpuram )

rākam - ......; tāḷam - .....
taṉattattat taṉattattat taṉattattat taṉattattat
taṉattattat taṉattattat ...... taṉatāṉa
kaṭattippaṟ ṟeṉappaṟṟik karuttuṟṟuk kaḷittiṭṭuk
kayaṟkaṭpoṟ piṇiccitrat ...... taṉamātar
kalikkuṭpaṭ ṭaṟakkattic calittukkaṭ ṭaḷiccoṟpoyt
tirikkuṭpaṭ ṭaṟaccettiṭ ...... ṭuyirpōṉāl
eṭuttukkoṭ ṭiṭakkaṭṭip paṭatteṭṭat taṇaṟṟaṭṭak
koḷutticcuṟ ṟavarppaṟṟaṟ ...... ṟavarpōmuṉ
iṇakkippat timicceccip patattippaṟ ṟukikkuccoṟ
ṟamiḻkkoṟṟap pukaḻceppit ...... tirivēṉō
aṭittiṭṭup puṭittuppoṟ pataccorkkat taṉiccuṟṟiṭ
ṭalippuppaṟ ṟeṉaccoṟṟiṭ ...... ṭaṟucūri
aṭittucceṟ ṟiṭittuppoṭ ṭeḻapporppup paṭakkuttiṭ
ṭalittuccuṟ ṟalitteṟṟuk ...... kaṭalmāyap
puṭittiṭṭup paṭikkuṭceṟ ṟaṭappukkuk katattukkak
kayiṟkokkip paṭakkuttip ...... poruvōṉē
puṉattiṟpoṟ kuṟattikkup puṇarkkottap pacappettip
pūṇarkkaccip paticcokkap ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 327 ( kāñcīpuram )

rākam - ....; tāḷam - .......
taṉattattat taṉattattat taṉattattat taṉattattat
taṉattattat taṉattattat ...... taṉatāṉa
karuppaṟṟip paruttokkat tarikkuṟṟiṭ ṭuruppeṟṟuk
karuttiṟkaṭ poruṭpaṭṭup ...... payilkālaṅ
kaṇakkiṭṭup piṇakkiṭṭuk katittiṭṭuk kotittiṭṭuk
kayiṟṟiṭṭup piṭittiṭṭuc ...... camaṉāvi
perukkapput tiyiṟpaṭṭup puṭittukkak kiḷippiṟpoyp
piṇatticcuṭ ṭakattiṟpuk ...... kaṉivōrum
piṟattaṟcuṟ ṟamuṟṟuṟṟiṭ ṭaḻittuttok kaṟakkattup
piṟappuppaṟ ṟaṟacceccik ...... kaḻaltārāy
poruppukkarp purakkaccut taṉappoṟput tiṉippaccip
puṉakkoccik kuṟattattik ...... kiṉiyōṉē
puratticcuṭ ṭerittuppaṟ ṟalarkkuppoṟ patattuyppip
puṇarttappit taṉikkaṟpit ...... taruḷvōṉē
cerukkakkuk karikkuttic ceruppukkup piṭitteṟṟic
ciṉattiṭṭuc citittiṭṭup ...... porumvīrā
tiruttattiṟ pukaṟcuttat tamiḻccepput trayaccitrat
tirukkaccip paticcokkap ...... perumāḷē.

pāṭal 328 ( kāñcīpuram )

rākam - .....; tāḷam - ......
taṉattattat taṉattattat taṉattattat taṉattattat
taṉattattat taṉattattat ...... taṉatāṉa
kaṟukkappaṟ ṟuvarppiṭṭuc cirittuccaṟ ṟuṟukkikkaṭ
piṟakkiṭṭup paṭakkaṟpit ...... tiḷiñōrtaṅ
kaḻutticcik keṉakkaṭṭit taṉacceppup paṭakkuttiṭ
ṭurukkikkaṟ paḻikkappoṟ ...... peḻukātal
puṟappaṭṭuk kaḷikkakkaṟ putartippiṭ ṭarakkippoṟ
paṇikkaṭṭiṟ puṟattuṟṟup ...... puṇarmātar
poruttattit tavirttuccaṟ ṟirakṣittup purappappoṟ
patattippeṟ ṟirukkikkup ...... peruvēṉō
tiṟaṟkokkip paṭakkuttic cerukkikkok karittuccak
karikkupput tiraṟkuṟṟut ...... taḷipūṇac
ciṉattuppoṟ poruppippoṭ ṭeḻuttittik karittupput
tirattattiṟ cirittuṟṟup ...... palapēykaḷ
paṟikkappac ciṟiccikkaṭ kaṟikkuppic ciraccikkup
parappoykkaṭ ṭaṟappukkup ...... porutōṉē
paṇicceccit toṭiccitrap puyattukrap paṭiccattip
paṭikkaccip paticcokkap ...... perumāḷē.

pāṭal 329 ( kāñcīpuram )

rākam - pākēsrī; tāḷam - saṅkīrṇa cāpu (4 1/2)
taka-1, timi-1, takatakiṭa-2 1/2
tattat taṉatāṉa tattat ...... taṉatāṉa)
aṟṟik kiritēṭi attat ...... tilumāci
paṟṟit taviyāta paṟṟip ...... peṟuvēṉō
veṟṟik katirvēlā veṟpit ...... toḷicīlā
kaṟṟuṟ ṟuṇarpōtā kaccip ...... perumāḷē.

------------------------------------------------------------------------

pāṭal 330 ( kāñcīpuram )

rākam - pehāk; tāḷam - aṅkatāḷam (6)
takiṭa-1 1/2, takiṭa-1 1/2, takatimitaka-3
tattat tattat ...... taṉatāṉa
muṭṭup paṭṭuk ...... katitōṟum
muṟṟac cuṟṟip ...... palanāḷum
taṭṭup paṭṭuc ...... cuḻalvēṉic
caṟṟup paṟṟak ...... karutātō
vaṭṭap puṭpat ...... talamītē
vikkat takkat ...... tirupātā
kaṭṭat taṟṟat ...... taruḷvōṉē
kaccic cokkap ...... perumāḷē.
